# Agent sync board — bilateral monitor & update

**Purpose:** Any coding agent working this repo coordinates **here** (append-only logs + status) instead of routing every micro-update through the owner. The owner checks **one file** for progress after `git pull` or opening the repo.

**Constitution (both agents):**

1. **Read** this file at **session start** (after `AGENT_HANDOFF_XAU_GATE_ENTRY_TEMPLATE.md` §4.1 if doing XAU mission).
2. **Append** new entries under **§ Activity log** — never delete or rewrite another agent’s past entries; you may add a *follow-up* line.
3. **Update** **§ Quick status** (table + phase) when you complete a phase, unblock something, or discover a blocker.
4. **Ask for help** in **§ Needs / questions** with `@agent` style (e.g. “`@trading-strategy` please confirm threshold”) — the next agent that touches this file **answers by appending** under the same heading or in the log.
5. **Owner summary:** Keep **§ Owner — latest** to **one short paragraph** max; refresh when mission phase changes (so mrgeo sees progress at a glance).

**Technical detail:** This file is **git-tracked**. Conflict resolution: if merge conflict, keep both sides’ log entries; fix the summary table manually.

### 2026-07-07 UTC 23:59Z — claude-fable (PM) — cTrader window-off-screen incident + CORRECTED V1.0→V1.1 stats (16-0)

- **Incident:** owner couldn't open cTrader (icon in taskbar only). Diagnosed: window was positioned at Top=-21333px (off-screen), not minimized/hung — caused by repeated watchdog kill/relaunch cycles. Fixed via Win32 `MoveWindow` API (PowerShell) to reposition on-screen; disabled `DexterCtraderMcpWatchdog` task during the fix, re-enabled once cTrader + MCP confirmed healthy. Open lane position was never at risk (broker-side SL/TP always attached; only the desktop UI was affected, not the account).
- **Correction — a prior chat report this session was WRONG** (net -$7.36, 32W/18L, PF 0.97 "since V1.0"). Root cause: an ad-hoc diagnostic script called `get_deals` with `from`/`to` params — the Local MCP tool pages by `count` only and silently ignores `from`/`to` (see `dexter3/mcp_client.py::get_deals` docstring), so the script read a stale/wrong window. **Production code (`daily_governor`, `dexter3_pnl_backtest.py`) already avoids this correctly** (`get_deals(count=500)` + client-side timestamp filtering) — this was a diagnostic-script bug only, not a live-trading bug.
- **Verified correct numbers** (get_deals(count=500), filtered client-side, cross-checked against real balance delta $10595.50→$10649.19 = +$53.69, matches net almost exactly): **since V1.0 start (2026-07-07T14:54Z UTC / 21:54 Bangkok) to now: 16 closes, 16W/0L, net +$54.43.** Split: V1.0 window 12W/0L +$14.88 (avg win $1.24); V1.1 window 4W/0L +$39.55 (avg win $9.89 — ~8× bigger, consistent with the true-R-base fix letting winners run further).
- **Lesson for future diagnostics (and future agents):** when pulling `get_deals` ad-hoc, ALWAYS use the wrapper `Dexter3McpClient.get_deals(count=N)` and filter by timestamp client-side — never pass `from`/`to` directly to `call('get_deals', ...)`, it is silently a no-op filter.
- Sample is still small (16 closes, only 4 in V1.1) — directionally strong, not yet statistically proven. Continue accumulating before declaring PF>1 confirmed.

### 2026-07-08 UTC 00:54Z — claude-fable (PM/Opus) — CRITICAL fix: OM trail + time-stop were dead (openTime field)

- Owner audit request: "why did a position that WAS in profit ride back to negative?" Investigated (no changes until approved), found THE root cause: `basket_live._position_open_ts()` read `openTimestamp`/`open_ts`/`openedAt`/`ts`, but the live MCP returns the key as **`openTime`** → `aggregate_lane` reported `oldest_open_ts=None` on every real position. Two dead systems as a result: (1) OM peak-R ratchet reset to live_r EVERY tick (`_basket_runtime_for` treats null oldest_open_ts as a new basket) — the continuous-peak trail never banked a winner; (2) `time_stop_min` cap never fired (age always None).
- **Smoking gun:** position 649759566 peaked +0.82R (~+$13) at 19:18Z, trail never armed, rode to a full stop loss **-$15.84** at 00:39Z (~$28 give-back). Live log: peak_r==live_r every tick for 5+ hours. The two 19:55Z repair legs DID work correctly (closed +$4.81/+$4.72 via their own TPs) — repair path was never broken, only the trail.
- Fix (`63c8025`, owner-approved): add `openTime` first in the `_position_open_ts` fallback chain. One-line data-plumbing fix, additive, zero logic change, no other module touched. `_parse_iso_epoch` already handles the millis+Z value. Regression test pins the exact live field name+value. Verified end-to-end: ratchet now holds peak_r=0.82 as live_r falls to -0.10 vs resetting before. 650/650 tests. Tag `v1.2-dexter3-trailfix`.
- Deployed clean while broker FLAT + MCP healthy. Loop live 00:53:50Z. **Root-cause class = the same MCP-field-name-mismatch that bit us 3× before (get_spot_prices, deals `time`, R-base) — future agents: ALWAYS verify extractor keys against a real get_positions/get_deals dump, never trust mock field names.**
- Next (fable): verify oldest_open_ts is non-null on the FIRST live position + watch the trail actually fire a close_all on a reversing winner.

### 2026-07-09 UTC 17:40Z — claude-fable (Fable 5) — MCP ZOMBIE ROOT CAUSE FOUND + FIXED: session leak (owner /goal: no restarts, stable)

- **ROOT CAUSE (proven with data, not theory): MCP session leak.** The watchdog log carries **2,940 lines = 2,940 DISTINCT session ids** — every client ever created a new session and NEVER deleted it. Today alone: 645 watchdog runs (schtask every 2 min) = 645 leaked sessions + every loop/audit/replay client → **61 zombie events today**. The cTrader plugin's session table exhausts → handler 404s everything → "zombie". App restart "cures" it precisely because it wipes the session table — hence the endless sick→restart→sick cycle. Degrading latency before death observed live (61ms → 6,462ms → 404).
- **DELETE support proven live:** created session `ab38939e` → HTTP DELETE → calling with that sid returns 404 (server-side removal confirmed) while fresh sessions work. The plugin was always capable of hygiene; no client ever asked.
- **Fix (zero-churn architecture, commit pending):**
  1. `Dexter3McpClient.close_session()` + **automatic atexit registration** on first initialize (`DEXTER3_MCP_SESSION_AUTODELETE=0` to disable) — every consumer (loops, tally, replay, ad-hoc) becomes leak-free with zero call-site changes; the zombie-retry path now DELETEs the dead session instead of abandoning it.
  2. `scripts/ctrader_mcp_client.py` (CtraderMcpClient): unconditional `atexit.register(_delete_session)` — covers watchdog + all script users.
  3. `scripts/ctrader_mcp_watchdog.py`: explicit try/finally `_delete_session()` — the single biggest churner (645/day) becomes session-neutral **on its very next schtask run, no restart of anything needed** (schtask executes the script from disk).
- Tests: 2 new hygiene tests; focused suite **244 passed**. Soak test running: 40 sequential fresh clients (the exact churn pattern that killed the plugin) against the live MCP.
- **Stability instrumentation armed:** persistent monitor on watchdog log `ok:false` — any future zombie event is caught and timestamped. Honest claim protocol: "stable" = zombie rate drops from ~61/day to ~0 over a multi-hour window; measurement ongoing, will be reported. Live loops (Fable 22000 / Grok 4804) still run pre-fix client code (leak ≈1 session per process — negligible vs 645/day) and inherit hygiene at their next natural restart.
- Watchdog `--restart` self-heal stays as defense-in-depth; expectation after this fix is it stops firing.

### 2026-07-11 UTC 06:05Z — claude-fable (Fable 5, Opus) — ROOT CAUSE of unmanaged VM losers: OM blind to PnL (gap #3) — FIXED

- Owner pushed deeper on the -$18/-$20 unmanaged shorts. Journal proof (Fri session): `BASKET action=hold legs=1 agg_r=0.0 pnl=0.0` on EVERY tick while the position was actually -$18. OM was ticking (95 ok vs 17 reconcile-timeouts) but fed pnl=0.0.
- **ROOT CAUSE:** OpenAPI ProtoOAPosition has NO netProfit → `basket_live._position_pnl` returns None → `aggregate_lane` unreliable=True → `decide_basket_action` HOLD (`unreliable_pnl_snapshot`) forever. VM lanes could ENTER but never MANAGE — every loser ran to the broker SL untrailed/uncut. This crippled EVERY VM trade, not just the weekend one. On PC (local MCP) netProfit was present, so this was silent until the VM cutover.
- **FIX (`f0066ef`, deployed surgically to VM):** `get_positions()._enrich_positions_with_live_pnl` computes netProfit from the daemon's live spot (SHORT@ask, LONG@bid) × volume × `_USD_POINT_VALUE_PER_UNIT` (XAUUSD=1.0, contract-derived: 1oz×$1=$1). Best-effort, never raises: no fresh spot (market closed/stale/unknown symbol) → no netProfit → basket stays unreliable→HOLD (correct — nothing to manage on a dead feed). 2 new tests; 229 green. **Live-verifiable Monday at open** (spot frozen over the weekend now, so the enrichment currently no-ops → still hold, which is correct).
- **VM working-tree is MESSY (queued cleanup, NOT touched now):** `git status` on VM shows uncommitted fibo deletions + staged empirical_stats.py/executor.py + modified bridge_server/board. I deployed the fix surgically (`git checkout origin -- dexter3/openapi_client.py`) to avoid disturbing the running lanes/token. A dedicated `git reset --hard origin` cleanup should happen when broker is flat + verified (protect .env.local + data/runtime/ctrader_token_state.json — confirm both gitignored first).
- Monday priority order: (1) verify PnL fix live-manages a position (this fix), (2) weekend-flatten rule, (3) reconcile-timeout (17/112 ticks failed at 5s — bump daemon client timeout or lighten OM's position read), (4) VM working-tree cleanup, (5) deals-label gap, (6) GPT-5.6 co-founder P0s.

### 2026-07-11 UTC — codex — OpenAPI OM audit: removed unnecessary deal-list latency from hot path

- **Finding:** the 5s→12s timeout patch reduced symptoms but `get_positions()` still made daemon `_mode_reconcile` call `ProtoOADealListReq` on every OM/entry/close preflight. OM never consumes those historical deals; that heavy broker call has its own 15s server timeout and was therefore a remaining source of blind `om_lane_read_failed` ticks.
- **Patch (local, not deployed):** `get_positions`, pending-order reads, and close preflight now send `include_deals=false`; daemon skips `ProtoOADealListReq` only for that explicit hot path. `get_deals()` retains `include_deals=true` and the labeled historical order join. This is a latency/risk reduction only — no entry, sizing, exit, or PnL decision changed.
- **Proof:** `tests/test_dexter3_openapi_client.py` **68 passed** and `py_compile` for client+daemon passed. Regression test asserts the OM position path sends `include_deals=false`.
- **Fable deploy handoff:** surgical deploy only `dexter3/openapi_client.py` and `dexter3/openapi_daemon.py` from this commit on the dirty VM; do not pull/reset/restart a lane while the Friday position is protected over market close. After the next safe restart, record reconcile p50/p95 and count of `om_lane_read_failed`; success is no deal-list request on OM ticks and no PnL-blind hold when a fresh spot exists.

### 2026-07-15 UTC ~01:50Z — claude-fable (Fable 5, PM) — owner flagged 2 bad live entries; forensics found 2 REAL bugs + 2 design gaps (report only, nothing changed yet)

- Owner (chart read): grok sold a minor support after a green close w/ wick, dead in <1 min; fable bought a minor resistance after two long upper wicks. Both correct — journal forensics:
- **BUG #1 — cross-lane vanish reconcile poisons the learner.** Grok logged `vanish_reconciled position_id=652554512 ... pnl=0.0` at 01:15:20Z for **fable's position that was still OPEN** (fable OM held it at 01:15:30Z, pnl=-13.97). ALL 5 vanish events since 07-14 were written by the GROK unit — several for fable-owned positions; fable has written 0. Mechanism: `reconcile_vanished_lane_positions` appears not to filter journal entry rows by the lane's own label → the other lane "vanishes" them with pnl=0.0 (no closing deals exist for an open position), and the dedup marker then swallows the REAL close later. Learner + lane tally have been fed fake pnl=0.0 closes since the 07-11 deploy (explains tally-vs-broker drift, e.g. fable 07-14 tally -12.85 vs broker -17.07, and the weird `net=+0.00 L=1` rows).
- **BUG #2 — governor daily-loss window crosses the UTC midnight boundary.** Grok position 652509725: entered 00:00:34Z, governor fired `daily loss cap (effective=$-16.32, cap=-$15)` at 00:00:54Z → position closed 19s after entry (-$0.91). The "daily" realized included 07-14 losses just after rollover; and the cap check ran AFTER entry (enter→instant-flatten = spread+commission burned). Grok has been `governor_loss_stopped` ALL day off yesterday's losses.
- **GAP #3 — grok has no quality floor:** the sell's own committee said p_win 0.447 (sub-coinflip), leader_score 0.056 (fable's gate min is 0.18); grok bypasses v16 by design → enters anyway (participation-first mandate).
- **GAP #4 — fable's flagged buy came through the V18 B-tier scout override:** score 0.162 < 0.18 min, `pass_b_tier_scout` admitted it at half size (v18-size 3.67->1.84) — straight into the two-wick resistance; floating -3.3R within 6 min. B-tier is doing what it was configured to do (collect sub-threshold samples) — whether that experiment is worth its cost in this regime = owner decision.
- Neither lane's committee reads candle anatomy / local S-R (Entry Sharpness Score exists in the MAIN system only, never ported to dexter3) — the owner's eyes are currently a strictly better entry filter than the committee at levels.
- **Proposed fix order (awaiting owner):** (1) vanish label-isolation fix + backfill-safe dedup (sonnet + tests — data integrity first); (2) governor: clamp realized window to the UTC day + check cap BEFORE entry; (3) grok minimum leader_score floor (soft, env-tunable); (4) owner call on `DEXTER3_V18_B_TIER_ENABLED`.

### 2026-07-23 UTC — claude (Opus 4.8) — dpull "find a better fix" (owner: อย่าด่วนสรุป): close-stop family tested → all fail robust bar, but a real REGIME edge catalogued

- Owner pushed past the M1-confirm/trend-filter rejection to find a smarter fix. Observed live trade pid 655111580 was WICK-stopped (M5 low 4143.28 < sl 4144.14 on a bar that CLOSED 4144.34 ABOVE, then recovered). Cross-checked the convex-replay's own MAE study: tail runners' worst pre-run dip is p90 0.86R, never >1R → the limit -0.5R entry's ~0.5R stop wicks out ~37.5% of 3R+ runners. Hypothesis: a CLOSE-based stop keeps all entries (edge volume intact) but grants wick-immunity.
- **Built as additive replay instrument** (`_simulate_convex(close_stop, close_stop_hard_mult)` + `--convex-close-stop[-cap]`, default off, live untouched; 23 daytrend/convex tests green). Matrix vs base (limit-0.5R × convex a2.0 gb3.0 h24), 3 windows:
  - **base (intrabar):** 6k +39.8/+57.4 · 10k +11.7/+117.1 · 14k +1.3/+96.5 — robust both segments.
  - **CLOSE-STOP (uncapped):** validate EXPLODES (+151/+203/+145, PF up to 1.96, WR 20→28%) but derive goes **-41.8/-54.1** on 10k/14k. **Regime-split: great in ranging, blows up in the crash.**
  - **CLOSE-STOP-CAP0.5 (-1.5R backstop):** derive still -8.3 (10k), and the cap KILLS the validate benefit (+40.7/+93.6/+46.0, below base) — the -1.5R backstop catches the runner's own 0.86R dip too. **REJECTED.**
  - **CLOSE-STOP-CAP1.0 (-2R backstop):** derive -16.4/-10.2 (10k/14k). **REJECTED.**
- **Why no cap works:** in a crash a wick below SL is NOT noise — it's the real move continuing; holding through it eats >1R, and any cap tight enough to stop that also amputates runners' normal dips. Price alone can't separate "ranging noise wick" from "crash-start wick" → needs regime detection the system lacks (and which has killed prior experiments).
- **VERDICT: no variant beats base robustly → dpull UNCHANGED; the 3 SLs on 07-22 are variance (confirmed across 5 variants / 3 families now).** CATALOGUED LEAD: uncapped close-stop nearly DOUBLES the take in ranging markets (= the current validate regime, +90-200R) — a conditional edge worth deploying IF/when robust regime detection exists. Instrument kept in the harness for that day. Logs VM /tmp/dpcs_*.log, /tmp/dpcap_*.log.

### 2026-07-25 UTC ~04:40Z — claude (Opus 4.8) — fable −9.56 forensics (NO defect) + 🚨 vp deep-dive finding: 155 enter decisions → 6 fills

- **Owner asked why the last closed fable trade lost.** `pid=655715920` (`hunt_h1_context`, ny): BUY 17:15Z, broker-verified fill (vol 3.0, geometry_ok, risk $8.75, SL 4061.32 / TP 4078.11), SL-hit 18:05Z = **−9.56 ≈ 1.09R**. **VERDICT: no malfunction.** The day's bias was UP (day-high 4082; daytrend was buying "ยืนเปิด 18h green resume" pullbacks all afternoon) and fable bought WITH the H1 uptrend — committee `h1_context vote 0.51-1.00 ×w1.40`, conviction 0.20-0.25 = a correctly-aligned trade. It lost because the **pullback was 14-19 pts deep while the structural SL sat only ~2.9 pts under entry** → wicked out before the trend resumed. That is trend-pullback variance, and it matches the bible's recurring "tight structural stops get wicked out" pattern (dpull intrabar, fable sweep wick) — a possible future lever (ATR-floor on SL when the day's pullback amplitude is large), NOT a bug.
- **Bonus proof the overhaul did NOT make fable over-trade:** since 15:24Z fable produced **51 `enter` DECISIONS but only 1 real fill** (`entry_executed`, this trade). 43/51 carried `leader_score` < the 0.18 gate. The "ทุก M5 close ต้องมี action" participation design writes an intent to the JOURNAL; the gate chain (min_leader_score + limit + M1-confirm) is what decides orders. Amplifying `W_H1` changes DIRECTION SELECTION, not entry frequency.
- **🚨 THE BIG FINDING (vp deep-dive step 1, broker truth + journal):** vp's whole +22.72 comes from **ONE setup** — `vp_lvn_rejection`, n=6, WR 50%, **payoff 2.9:1** (3 wins avg **+11.57** via the convex ride: close_all_in_profit / om_convex_time_stop / governor_target_lock; 3 losses avg **−4.00**, all broker_side_close). That geometry — small controlled losses, big convex winners — is exactly what fable lacks. **BUT: `decide_vp` has THREE setups and the lane journaled 155 `enter` decisions all-time — `vp_lvn_rejection` 75, `vp_poc_reversion` 72, `vp_hvn_break_retest` 6 — while the broker shows only 6 fills, ALL of them lvn_rejection. `vp_poc_reversion` is 72-for-0.** So ~96% of vp's entry decisions never become orders, and one entire setup has never traded once.
- **CORRECTION to prior memory:** the note that vp wins via "poc_reversion, selective" is **WRONG** — live it is lvn_rejection only; poc_reversion is fully blocked. (Verify-before-reporting rule earning its keep again.)
- **NOT yet diagnosed (do not guess):** WHAT blocks them. The producer is fine (it decides; `_enter`'s RR floor `MIN_REWARD_RISK` passes before an enter is emitted), so the killer is downstream — the vp unit is "day-open bias × **limit** × convex", so `_enter`'s `entry_type="market"` gets converted to a LIMIT intent (`_service_vp_limit_intent`) that can simply never fill, plus prem-cap / bias-skip / no-trade-window. vp `enter` rows carry NO gate evidence in `features_json` (the `c50c8e2` resync stamps the fable/hunt path only) → the journal cannot answer it, and the market is CLOSED (weekend) so no live logs can be captured now.

### 2026-07-25 UTC ~05:30Z — claude (Opus 4.8) — vp entry-funnel INSTRUMENTATION deployed (`87f35ff`) — the 72-for-0 answer lands at reopen

- Owner: "เขียน instrument ไว้รอเลย". Built + deployed during the weekend close (safest window) so the next open produces the causes automatically.
- **Why it was unanswerable:** `insert_decision` runs BEFORE the gate chain; `c50c8e2`'s post-gate resync only helps if a gate STAMPED something into `decision.features` — the hunt path does (`v16_entry_quality`), but the **vp branch (`vp_lane.vp_entry_gate`, shadow_runner ~L2126) only replaces a local `quality` var and stamps nothing**, and the accumulated `status` string (which names every downstream branch) only ever reached the log line. So the DB knew a decision existed and nothing about its fate.
- **Deployed (`87f35ff`, hash-verified DIFF_EMPTY_MATCH + py_compile, 6 lanes restarted clean, 0 errors) — pure observability, no sizing/gating/order path touched:**
  - `features_json.entry_outcome` — stamped ONCE after the whole gate chain, derived from the accumulated cycle `status`, so no branch can be missed: `filled` / `limit_intent` / `blocked:<gate>` / `no_exec`. (`governor_*_bypassed` correctly does NOT count as blocked.)
  - `features_json.intent_fate` — the synthetic-limit intent's FINAL fate, stamped from the fast tick: `entered` / `expired` / `killed_break` / **`replaced`**. `_service_vp_limit_intent` now takes an optional `journal`.
  - `DecisionJournal.merge_decision_features` — read-modify-write. **This was the real corruption risk:** the fast tick holds no copy of the features, and `update_decision_features` rewrites the column wholesale, so a naive fate stamp would have DESTROYED the gate evidence the resync had just written. Pinned by a test.
  - `ops/dexter3_vp_funnel.py` — per-setup funnel query; pre-instrumentation rows reported separately and never folded into the percentages.
- **PRIME SUSPECT for vp_poc_reversion 72-for-0 (found while reading the live path, NOT yet proven):** shadow_runner ~L2160 — *"Newest allowed signal REPLACES any pending intent"*. vp decides on every M5 close, so a pending limit intent can be evicted every 5 minutes before it is ever touched. If the funnel comes back dominated by `intent_fate=replaced`, the bottleneck is **entry-mechanics/frequency, not edge** — and the fix would be about intent retention (e.g. don't evict an unexpired same-side intent), not about the producer. If instead it is dominated by `blocked:vp_dayopen_bias` or `expired`, those are different diseases with different medicines. **Measure first — do not pre-commit to a fix.**
- 19 new tests, **1356 dexter3 green**. Tool verified end-to-end against the real journal (correctly reports all 155 historical rows as "not instrumented" — positive control passed).
- **AT THE SUNDAY REOPEN (21:00Z), run:** `python3 ops/dexter3_vp_funnel.py` on the VM (add `--since 2026-07-25` for instrumented-only). Then judge whether `vp_poc_reversion` deserves unblocking via an isolated 2-3 window harness (bible step 3) BEFORE touching any gate. Memory: [[project_vp_deepdive_2026_07_25]].

No proven edge yet — **but 89% of stop-outs land in [−1.3,−0.7]R, so this is an ENTRY-QUALITY deficit, not a risk-control or exit-tuning one.**
- **🔦 THE EDGE (deployed today): SL ≥ 1.2× true range.** <1.2×TR = **−0.398R** (N=30) vs ≥1.2×TR = **+0.116R** (N=25); swing **+0.51R/trade**. Controls passed 4/4 (within fable alone, within dtr alone, both time halves). Mechanism measured not fitted: tight cohort hits TP **6–8%** vs **27–29%** at comparable planned RR — the opposite of a random walk. Monotone dose-response across 4 buckets (−0.735/−0.140/+0.008/+0.253). **This is the ONE root behind four separate per-lane "wick-out" fixes** (dpull intrabar, fable sweep wick-SL, dpull-cs close-stop, the 07-25 −9.56 loss I wrongly called "just variance").
- **DEPLOYED `3bc820d`** (hash-verified DIFF_EMPTY_MATCH, py_compile OK, fable+daytrend restarted, 6 lanes active, 0 errors): new pure `dexter3/stop_floor.py` + ONE central wiring point in `run_symbol_cycle` (NOT in the 4 producers — deliberately avoiding the "wiring lives in N places" defect class). **RR preserved** (TP scaled by the same factor — the winning cohort was wide-stop *at comparable RR*, and every TP shortening tested loses). `DEXTER3_SL_FLOOR_MAX_ABS` bounds the widening so a volatile bar cannot push 1-oz dollar risk past `MIN_VOLUME_RISK_ABS_CAP_USD` and silently turn the trade into a refusal. `features["sl_floor"]` stamped even when it does not bind = **proof-of-execution**. Live on **fable + daytrend only** (mult 1.3 / max_abs 10 — measured floors 4.38 and 5.88, both well under the $12 cap); **vp/scalp/dpull-cs/chf are untouched CONTROLS**. 32 new tests, 1388 green.

- **CONFIRMED BUGS (I verified #1 and #3 myself):** `dexter3:dpull` is a strict prefix of `dexter3:dpull-cs` (`.startswith` matcher) → contaminated the very A/B that retired dpull · dpull-cs's convex trail can never arm (R denominator rescaled 3.5× by its own backstop amend) · `shadow_runner.py:2645` `prefer_signal_tp = not _dpull_producer_enabled()` omits dpull-cs → wrong TP, so that A/B differs in 3 dimensions not 1 · governor close-all is fire-once · a `get_deals` outage silently disables the daily loss cap.

### 2026-07-25 UTC ~11:10Z — claude (Opus 4.8) — 3 confirmed audit bugs FIXED + grok stage-1 removal (`059ac10`, `87f464a`, deployed)

- All fixes are **behaviour-neutral for fable and daytrend**, which are FROZEN for the SL-floor experiment (pinned by a test). Deployed hash-verified (DIFF_EMPTY_MATCH + py_compile), 6 lanes restarted, **0 errors**, 1420 dexter3 green.
- **(1) CRITICAL label-family collision FIXED.** `label_matches_family` was a bare `startswith()`, so root `dexter3:dpull` matched `dexter3:dpull-cs:canary` — a different LANE, not a version. dpull read dpull-cs's deals/positions as its own (premature LOSS_STOPPED on a peer's losses, foreign legs in its basket) and **it contaminated the very A/B those lanes existed to run**. New rule: `:` is the boundary; `-` is honoured ONLY when a version token (`v`+digit) follows — keeps grok's legacy `dexter3:grok-v1.0:scalper` matching while rejecting `-cs:canary`, with no lane name hardcoded. Applied to `executor` AND `basket_live` (which duplicates the rule by convention); a test pins them in agreement. Verified across all 9 live labels: only the buggy pair changed.
- **(2) Self-inflicted fail-CLOSED in today's market-state gate FIXED.** `_iso_to_epoch` returns sentinel `0.0` on an unparseable bar ts; `0.0 + 300` fed the gate a ~1.7e9s bar age → bogus `stale_feed` → **halts entries on every lane running the gate**, inverting market_state's documented fail-OPEN contract. **This would have silently voided the SL-floor experiment.** Now passes `None` (unknown age) → verdict degrades to open.
- **(3) dpull-cs wrong TP FIXED.** `prefer_signal_tp = not _dpull_producer_enabled()` omitted `_dpull_cs_producer_enabled()`, so dpull-cs kept the signal TP (day extreme) instead of the far cap its own `VP_FAR_TP_R=12` configures — truncating the convex ride the lane exists to capture and making its A/B differ in TP placement too. Same missed-second-wiring-site class as the chf label bug.
- **GROK REMOVAL — stage 1 done (owner: "ไม่ต้องการ grok แล้ว").** `ops/dexter3-grok.service` deleted from repo and from `/etc/systemd/system`; unit no longer exists on the VM. **Stage 2 (~71 grok references inside `shadow_runner`'s live run loop) is DEFERRED ON PURPOSE** — that is the module fable/daytrend are frozen in, and this repo's own fibo lesson is that a removal of that size is a live-wired refactor, not cleanup. Do it when the freeze lifts, with behaviour-neutrality proven by tests.
- **BONUS — the "broker truth" tool was itself broken and is FIXED.** `ops/dexter3_lane_tally.py::_LANE_FAMILIES` listed only grok/vp/fable, so **dtr, dpull, dpull-cs, scalp and chf all bucketed as "other"** — a ledger that cannot name its own live lanes, and a structural cause of the cherry-picked PnL prose the board had to retract. Every lane is now listed (longest-prefix-first) and buckets through the boundary-aware matcher; grok's history buckets as "retired". **The tally now prints per-lane WR/PF for every lane for the first time.**

### 2026-07-25 UTC ~12:00Z — claude (Opus 4.8) — ALL HIGH-severity audit defects now fixed (`3235878`, deployed)

- Owner: "อย่าเหลืออะไรไม่เวิร์กไว้ค้างคา ผ่าตัดให้จบ". Round 2 closes the three remaining HIGH bugs. Deployed hash-verified (DIFF_EMPTY_MATCH + py_compile), 6 lanes restarted, **0 errors**, **1427 dexter3 green**.
- **(A) dpull-cs had NO PROFIT TRAIL AT ALL — fixed.** The lane sizes to a SOFT stop then amends the broker SL out to a 2.5× backstop the software close-stop owns; `_lane_actual_risk_usd` derived the R base from that WIDENED broker SL, inflating the denominator ~3.5×. A genuine +2.0R move reported `peak_r` **0.57**, so the convex trail could never arm at `ARM_R=2.0` (it needed a **7.0 soft-R** move inside a 120-min cap). Every winner rode to the time stop and **every journaled `live_r`/`peak_r`/`floor_r` for this lane is understated 3.5×** (treat historical dpull-cs R values accordingly). Fix threads the soft entry-time distance via `_soft_stop_pts()`, which returns 0.0 unless `DEXTER3_OM_CONVEX_CLOSE_STOP` is set — **every other lane byte-identical**.
- **(B) Governor close-all was FIRE-ONCE — fixed.** The latch was set before the close loop, so `newly_triggered` could never be true again that UTC day; inside the loop a `get_positions` failure only `continue`d and `execute_close_all`'s partial result was journaled but never checked. One transient hiccup at the wrong moment turned the daily loss cap into an **advisory** (entries blocked, open basket running past the cap on broker SL alone). The latch still fires once; only the CLOSE retries, gated on a verified `close_all_done`. (Verified the real return value is `"closed_all"` rather than assuming the string.)
- **(C) A `get_deals` outage silently disabled the daily cap — fixed.** The cached total cannot grow while closes keep landing, so `effective_pnl` freezes and neither the close-all trigger nor the pre-entry refusal can fire — and it logged **exactly once ever**, so an all-day outage produced one line. Now re-logged on a bounded 300s interval, and a never-successful read is escalated as `NO CACHE EVER — daily cap is BLIND` so a fabricated 0.0 is never read as "no losses today".
- **Freeze integrity preserved:** all three are behaviour-neutral for fable/daytrend — (A) is gated on a close-stop env they do not set, (B)/(C) only alter failure paths. The SL-floor experiment is intact.

### 2026-07-25 UTC ~13:30Z — claude (Opus 4.8) — 3 MED audit defects fixed (`52d31a5`, deployed). Evidence rungs stated.

Now mirrors its sibling `amend_lane_sl_tp`: settle 2s, let the authoritative re-read decide. **Safety property preserved and pinned by a test** — when the stop really is absent the position is still closed rather than left naked.
- **(2) A broker-REFUSED amend reported `verified=True`.** `verify_entry_snapshot` only proved the stop was on the correct SIDE of entry, never that it was where the caller asked — so a refused widen verified because the OLD tight stop still satisfied `sl < entry < tp`. **This is how dpull-cs's backstop could log "amended" while the position silently kept its soft stop, burn its 3 retries and give up permanently — reinstating the exact wick stop-out the backstop exists to prevent, and quietly defeating today's R-base fix.** `expect_sl`/`expect_tp` are optional (entry-time callers unchanged) and `amend_lane_sl_tp` now passes the requested prices.
- **(3) A spot-read failure vanished silently.** Not just lost observability: with no `netProfit` the basket degrades to `unreliable` and the OM **HOLDS**, disabling the convex trail, close-stop, time stop and close-all-in-profit for that tick — a persistent outage was an hour of exit-blindness indistinguishable from an unknown symbol. Now stamps `pnl_source="spot_unavailable"` + the error, mirroring the staleness path beside it; still never fabricates a PnL.

### 2026-07-25 UTC ~14:20Z — claude (Opus 4.8) — 🚨 THE LIMIT LAYER WE DEPLOYED IS NOT THE ONE WE PROVED (`8e8ae12`, fix built OFF-by-default)

- Owner asked where `HUNT_LIMIT_DIP_R` 0.4/0.5 came from, suspecting a lazily hardcoded magic number. **The VALUES are clean** — the board records a real sweep (3 exits × 3 dips × 2 segments, 17/18 cells improved; `limit0.4/w6` improved **16/16 across two windows, both segments**, board:1054/1061) and dpull's 0.5 is the dtcap matrix winner (board:1240). **The laziness is one level down, in the implementation.**
- **THE FINDING:** `scripts/dexter3_entry_position_replay.py::_limit_fill` (L169-196) is called **per signal, independently** — every signal gets its OWN limit that lives the full `window_bars` (w6 = 6 M5 bars = 30 min = the deployed `TTL_MIN=30`). The live loop instead keeps **ONE global slot** `state["vp_limit_intent"]` and lets each new M5 signal **overwrite** the pending one (shadow_runner ~L2196: *"Newest allowed signal replaces any pending intent"*).
  ```
  replay : 6 signals -> 6 limits, each waits its full 30 min
  live   : 6 signals -> 1 limit,  5 destroyed before they can fill
  ```
  **So the 16/16-cell proof describes a system we do not run.** A single-variable shortcut instead of a collection — never itself measured — and the leading explanation for `vp_poc_reversion`'s **72 enter decisions → 0 broker fills** (whole vp lane: 155 → 6).
- **Built `DEXTER3_LANE_INTENT_KEEP_UNEXPIRED`** — when set, a pending intent for this symbol whose TTL has not elapsed is no longer destroyed; the newer signal is dropped and journaled as `no_exec/intent_kept_prev` so the funnel counts it. Restores the semantics the replay actually measured.
- **DEFAULT OFF and enabled NOWHERE — verified 0 units carry it.** It materially raises fill frequency, and fable/daytrend are inside the frozen SL-floor measurement; enabling it now would void that. **Gate to enable:** either the funnel returns `intent_fate=replaced` as the dominant fate (confirming the mechanism on live data), or the freeze lifts.
- **Evidence rungs.** Rung 1 (observed): replay is per-signal (read `_limit_fill` + its single call site at L419); live is one slot (read L2196-2251); the sweep provenance (board + `git log -S`); 1446 dexter3 green; DIFF_EMPTY_MATCH + py_compile on the VM; 6 lanes active, 0 errors; `KEEP_UNEXPIRED` count across all units = **0**. Rung 2 (code written, tests pass, never executed live): the keep-unexpired branch itself. **Still a HYPOTHESIS, not proven:** that clobbering is what caused 72-for-0 — the funnel measures it at the next open.
- **METHOD LESSON (generalize):** when a replay proves a layer, check that the LIVE implementation has the same *state model*, not just the same parameters. Same `dip_r`, same TTL, same producer — and still a different system, because the harness held N pending orders and production holds one. Worth auditing the other proven-then-ported layers the same way.

### 2026-07-26 UTC ~00:10Z — claude (Opus 4.8) — 🔬 REPLAY vs LIVE RECONCILED (4 parallel audits). Full detail: auto-memory `project_replay_vs_live_truth_2026_07_25`.

- **THE ONE-LINE ANSWER: replay was never wrong — it simulated a DIFFERENT MACHINE.** It tested a system with no daily governor, no live gate chain, unlimited concurrent limit intents, unlimited concurrent positions, zero cost on tight stops, flat $12/R sizing and default committee weights. Production has every one of those constraints.
- **🔴 BIGGEST FINDING — the governor amputates the convex edge.** Canary lanes: 1R ≈ $4 and `DAILY_TARGET_USD=15` ⇒ TARGET_LOCKED at **+3.75R counting FLOATING PnL**, which closes every lane position at market. Convex `ARM_R=2.0` + ~3R giveback means the trail's floor only turns positive near peak ≈3R, and the proof's entire net comes from the **16.2% of trades reaching ≥3R (mean MFE 6.11R, max 20.64R)**. Live caps that tail at ~3.75R and pays every −1R loser in full. **With $10 already realized the lock fires at +1.25R — below the arm threshold — so the trail is mathematically unreachable and the lane runs a hidden ~1.25R fixed TP.** Ledger corroboration: `governor_target_lock` = 14.7% of gross profit (N=4) vs `om_convex_time_stop` N=2 — **the governor IS the exit.**
- **💰 WHAT MAKES THE MONEY:** fixed broker TP **66.5%** (16 trades, +$152.11) · governor lock 14.7% · convex 7.3% · ladder 7.1% · bank/stall 4.4%. **≈81% of profit comes from NOT managing the trade.** And **fable's exit stack has been INERT since 07-16 20:35Z** — 93% of its post-surgery closes are `broker_side_close`, all 17 `om_ladder_floor` closes predate the ladder→plain deploy. Every exit-geometry experiment since has tuned a component that does not fire.
- **🩺 THE PATTERN IS TWO MECHANISMS NEEDING OPPOSITE FIXES.** (a) a constant per-trade cost band replay prices at ZERO — live WR by stop width 11%→33%→41%→47%→**60% (+$50.17 at ≥5pt)**, within-lane 3/3 — so replay systematically rewards tightening stops, and every layer promoted this month tightened them; fix = geometry (SL floor). (b) semantic/state divergence, unfixable by any parameter — limit intent (1 slot vs N), sdzone zone engine (rebuilt every 5 min from 340 bars vs advanced continuously; **36 replay signals vs 29 live, 7 replay-only, 0 live-only ⇒ ~19% of the proven population structurally unreachable**). (c) noise (lane N: vp 6, scalp 2, dpull-cs 9, dpull 7).
- **⚠️ SELF-CONTRADICTION:** `MIN_VOLUME_RISK_ABS_CAP_USD` (9-12) refuses any trade whose 1-oz risk exceeds the cap — **a volatility-selective filter that deletes exactly the wide-stop trades live data shows are the winners**, while replay counted them at full weight.
- **🐛 NEW DEFECTS:** (1) **verbatim repeat of the hunter_brain incident** — `DEXTER3_HUNT_SWEEP_SL_ATR`/`_TP_RR` are set on the fable unit but read ONLY in `hunter_brain.py` = dead; the sweep FOLLOW edge was measured at SL 1×ATR/TP 1.5R and is traded live on swing-clamped SL + 1.2 RR floor — **proven edge ≠ deployed edge**. (2) the replay scores a **different committee** (`hunt_mode` reads `DEXTER3_HUNT_W_*` at import; the replay shell exports none ⇒ TOTAL 8.5 vs 8.0, sweep FADE vs FOLLOW, swing gate off vs on) and `leader_score = |Σ|/TOTAL` is the number every gate threshold is expressed in. (3) **entry fill price is discarded** ⇒ entry slippage is invisible and no forward test can falsify the replay's fill assumption. (4) prem-cap 0.25 was measured on M5 confirm, deployed on M1 confirm. (5) M1 zone-confirm is not scored at all in a default replay run. (6) sdzone bank dispatch non-exhaustive → silent fallback to the retired ladder. (7) replay `giveback_atr` uses one whole-series ATR (real lookahead). (8) max-hold: replay counts bars, live counts wall clock.
- **✅ CORRECTIONS:** fable does **not** stack 6 legs under the current config (plain trail ⇒ `basket_hold` suppresses entry while open; `allow_basket_legs=False`) — the earlier observation was cross-lane or pre-07-16. The min-volume clamp is **not** the expectancy leak (refuted: the uninflated cohort was worst at −0.30R). Both `hunt_mode.decide_hunt` and `sd_zones.decide_sdzone` are genuinely SHARED between replay and live — **the forks are in STATE and CONFIG, never in the maths.** An independent check also re-confirmed today's `soft_stop_pts` fix as correct.
- **NEXT (owner decision pending):** ranked remediation — (1) governor floating-lock vs convex lanes, (2) the dead sweep-geometry flags, (3) make the replay read the live committee env, (4) the abs-cap-deletes-winners contradiction, (5) enable `KEEP_UNEXPIRED` once the funnel confirms, (6) sdzone zone-engine persistence. **The SL-floor freeze on fable+daytrend still stands and still has 0 trades of evidence.**

### 2026-08-07 UTC ~12:30Z — claude (Opus 5) — 🧭 "DESIGN A REAL WINNER LANE" — I am NOT shipping one, and here is the measured reason: USTEC M5 price-path trading is a FAIR GAME MINUS COST. Three harness bugs caught by positive controls; nothing deployed

- Owner asked for a brand-new lane of my own design, not derived from the existing ones, a genuine winner. Method used (to avoid the day's failure mode of designing first and measuring after): **explore on the DERIVE half only, freeze, then test once on the untouched validate half.** The exploration never reached the validate stage — the derive half already answered the question.
- **Diagnostic on 5400 derive bars.** (A) Hour profile: bar range 22-32 overnight vs **13Z=65, 14Z=76, 15Z=54** — the US window is 2.5-3.5x, and signed drift is noise (±2 pts) in every hour. (B) **Volatility CLUSTERS, it does not mean-revert**: after a bottom-quartile 30-min range the next 30 min averages 56.9; after a top-quartile one, 130.4 (uncond 87.5) — a stable 2.3x separation, and it kills the coil→expansion premise. (C) **Return autocorrelation ≈ 0** (1-bar +0.0101, 3-bar +0.0127). (D) Excursion: within 30 min P(±5 both sides)=0.81, P(±20 both)=0.34, P(±40 both)=0.10.
- **Bracket study with a POSITIVE CONTROL (no-information entries must return ≈ −spread).** First run showed FADE +7.41/trade — **artifact**: the entry level was mis-assigned on the flip and the entry bar was being judged with a stop-before-target rule that mechanically punishes breakouts and rewards fades. With the entry bar excluded and both arms treated identically: **control −2.0 to −4.1/trade · breakout −0.83 to −1.63 · fade −3.48 to −7.84.** Breakouts beat random by roughly the spread and no more; **fading is measurably worse than random** — consistent with live evidence (sniper reversion cells negative, h3fade −9.51). **Conclusion, three independent ways: there is no directional information in USTEC M5 price paths at this resolution. That is why all 8 entry families failed — they were all coin-flips paying a spread.**
- **The one design with a real mechanism** (schedule-anchored expansion straddle: bracket the pre-event coil at 13:25Z, let the market pick the side, structural stop at the coil's far edge — direction-agnostic, monetising the only forecastable quantity, magnitude) **cannot be validated with available data: the broker caps USTEC M5 history at 13,999 bars (2026-05-28 → today, ~50 trading days), so a once-a-day design tops out at ~50 trades — ~30/20 split, below any honest two-segment bar.** Its derive slice was N=13 and its own inverted arm scored positive too (third harness bug) — unusable.
- **Deliberate non-delivery:** shipping a USTEC M5 pattern lane today would mean shipping a known EV≈0-minus-cost lane dressed as an edge — precisely what the owner asked me not to build. Honest options recorded for the owner's choice: **(1)** run the expansion design as a SHADOW (no-money) lane to accumulate forward N at zero risk and judge in ~2 months; **(2)** aim the search at XAUUSD, where every durable live positive in this repo lives (vp +23.58) while USTEC has produced none; **(3)** accept the regime finding and spend the effort on WHEN-not-to-trade, the one dimension never tested. Nothing deployed; 14 lanes untouched.

- **Design:** confirmed M5 pivots (k=3, confirmed at j+3, no lookahead) → leg P0→P1 ≥1.5×ATR14 → retracement r on the closed bar. **A1 (primary)** deep zone 0.5–0.786 + close in leg direction → market, SL beyond the leg origin −0.15×ATR, TP 1.618 extension. Secondaries A2 (0.382–0.618), A3 (TP=1.0), A4 (TP=2.618), B1 (fade the 1.618). 13,999 real USTEC M5 bars (2026-05-28→08-07, the broker cap), spread 1.5, exits from bar i+1 only, SL-first on both-touch, **three chronological segments with a sealed holdout** (50/30/20), points=dollars per the volume-floor law.

The Fibonacci level was contributing nothing.
- **A1's shape is the same regime signature as the other 8 families:** derive (late May–early July) negative, validate + holdout (mid-July on) positive. Fib levels do not escape mscalp's regime dependence; they inherit it.
- **Disclosed spec defect:** B1 as frozen produced ZERO signals (the leg-validity rule voids a leg the moment price extends past P1, so a close beyond the 1.618 extension can never coexist with a live leg). Amendment declared before running, exploratory, 2 extra comparisons — both landed under the N bar anyway. Engine positive control (ORACLE arm) +4984/+1926/+260 = harness sane; without it none of the above would be reportable.

- **Sharp-entry measurement (the answer to "จุดเข้าคม"): MAE separates cleanly ex-post — winners go 11-12 pts against you (p75 21-31), losers go 55-61, structural stop ~43 — but NOTHING observable at entry time predicts which is which.** Retracement depth, bar size, body fraction, wick fraction and session all flip between derive and validate. Only impulse-leg size moves MAE consistently and that is mechanical (bigger leg ⇒ bigger everything in ATR units); its reach-rate, the part that would pay, is 38/39% derive vs 60/31% validate = unstable. **So v3 tested only the stop — inventing an entry filter anyway would have been the 11th attempt.**

A ~1×ATR stop under a 1.618-extension target is a **long-gamma straddle**: it pays whenever the market moves, either way. It buys MAGNITUDE cheaply — the one quantity 08-07 found forecastable here — and **the Fibonacci level contributes nothing a coin flip could not.**

### 2026-08-08 UTC ~07:30Z — claude (Opus 5) — 💰 SYNTHESIS on broker truth: **94% of the loss comes from the INDEX lanes' STAKE, not from bad prediction.** Three buildable paths, nothing deployed

- Owner: *"จากบทเรียนทดสอบและ Forward test ที่ผ่านมาทั้งหมด น่าจะพอทำให้เป็นไปได้จริงไม่ว่าทางไหนสักทางไหม ต่อยอดอะไรได้บ้าง"*. Answered from the real ledger (944 deals since 2026-07-15), not from prose. Full write-up: `docs/handoff/WHAT_IS_ACTUALLY_POSSIBLE_20260808.md`.
- **SYSTEM = −$733.28 / 944 deals.** Positive: fable +23.64 (N=244) · mscalp-be +23.60 (N=30) · chf +11.84 · sniper-XAU +9.84 · scalp +2.37. Negative tail: **sniper-us30 −224.50 (N=20) · mscalp-be-base −197.20 (N=12) · mscalp −136.50 (N=76) · sniper-ustec −94.10 (N=16) · mscalp2 −35.40**.
- **🎯 THE REORGANISING FACT: five INDEX lanes = −$688 of the −$733 (94%). Eleven XAU lanes running 630 deals net ≈ ZERO (−$9 combined).** Avg loss per losing deal: XAU lanes **−$1.40…−3.19** vs USTEC lanes **−$14.65…−22.02**. Several index lanes share a producer with a break-even XAU cousin — so they are not worse strategies, they are **the same fair game played for 5-10× the stake**, because USTEC/US30 at the 1-unit volume FLOOR carries a notional XAU at 1 oz does not. **The system did not lose $733 by predicting badly; it lost it by betting big on a coin flip it had already proven was a coin flip.**
- **PATH A (strongest — arithmetic, not prediction): fix the STAKE, not the forecast.** A pre-trade check comparing `floor_volume × stop_distance × pip_value` against the per-trade risk budget, journaling a `floor_risk_exceeds_budget` skip. Removes ~94% of the live loss while deleting NO hypothesis and NO learning (signals keep being journaled) — this changes the stake, not the strategy set, so it is not the subtraction-from-fear the owner banned. Additive, env-gated, off by default.
- **PATH B: stop paying five times for one hypothesis.** Five mscalp-family lanes run the same producer; the 2×2 factorial was right AS MEASUREMENT and is now largely answered on live deals (mscalp-be +23.60/N=30 vs mscalp-be-base −197.20/N=12, consistent with replay's "BEW-on-BRK is the best cell"). Consolidating costs no knowledge. **Owner decision — those trials were opened by owner order.**
- **PATH C: cheap and rare, direction-agnostic** — the only design the data endorses. Every expensive expression of "collect the swing" failed **on cost, not on concept**; the buildable version is a once-daily breakout straddle at the measured-highest-volatility hour, no cap, structural stop = ~1.5 pts of cost per day instead of 1,079 firings. Same posture as the live `xaudaily`.
- **NOT possible on current evidence:** a profitable directional M5 scalper on USTEC — 10 entry families, 3 pre-registrations, 1 information campaign. Another entry filter is the definition of repeating ourselves.
- **Honest caveat recorded: fable's +$23.64 over 244 deals is +$0.10/deal = statistically ZERO.** The ledger licenses a claim about STAKE and COST (arithmetic); it licenses NO claim about prediction. Nothing deployed; all three paths need owner sign-off.

Every N-starved death (xaudaily N=30/19 · expansion straddle N=13 · fib B1/B2 N=13/8 · C4 N=28) was starvation in the WRONG MINE.
